Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on operators structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 268. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 410 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on operators structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 268. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 410. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on operators structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 458. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 690 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to operators theory, we decompose the problem into 458 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 690. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.

Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
